Usually, at this point we would show you the actual main decks of the undefeated players here at Grand Prix Lyon. We decided to switch things up for a change. Below are the complete pools of the undefeated players—in decklist format which allows you to load the pool on *Magic Online* where you can try building your own decks from the material at hand. Think you can find the 40 cards with which these players managed to go 9-0? 

Well, then check back tomorrow morning when we'll post the main decks!

#### Marc Lazaro – 9-0 Sealed Pool at Grand Prix Lyon 2015
